#### `ExponentialHistogramResult getExponentialHistogram(const std::string &name) const`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:160

#### `double getRate(const std::string &name) const`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:239
- Brief: Compute the per-second rate of change for name.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Metric name.
- Return: Per-second rate, or 0.0 when insufficient samples exist.
- Details: Rate is computed as (newest_value - oldest_value) / elapsed_seconds from the retained samples. Returns 0.0 if fewer than two samples exist. name Metric name. Per-second rate, or 0.0 when insufficient samples exist.

#### `SummaryResult getSummary(const std::string &name, const std::vector< double > &quantiles={0.5, 0.9, 0.95, 0.99}) const`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:127

- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Metric name.
  - `quantiles` (const std::vector< double > &): Quantiles to compute; each must be in [0, 1].
- Return: SummaryResult containing computed quantile_values, count, and sum. If no samples exist, quantile_values are 0.0 and count is 0.
- Details: name Metric name. quantiles Quantiles to compute; each must be in [0, 1]. SummaryResult containing computed quantile_values, count, and sum. If no samples exist, quantile_values are 0.0 and count is 0.

#### `void recordCardinality(const std::string &name, const std::string &value)`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:174
- Brief: Record a string value for a cardinality metric.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `value` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Details: Record Cardinality. Each unique value is counted once per metric name. name Metric name (e.g. "active_tenants"). value String value to track (e.g. a tenant ID). name Input parameter. value Input parameter. Calls: lock(), insert().

#### `void recordExponentialHistogram(const std::string &name, double value, double scale=2.0)`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:148
- Brief: Record a value for an exponential histogram metric.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `value` (double): Input parameter.
  - `scale` (double): Input parameter.
- Details: Record Exponential Histogram. Non-positive values are counted separately in zero_count and are not bucketed. The exponential base (scale) is locked in on the first call for a given metric name; subsequent calls with a different scale are silently ignored. name Metric name. value Observed value. scale Exponential base for bucket boundaries (must be > 1.0). Defaults to 2.0. name Input parameter. value Input parameter. scale Input parameter. Calls: lock(), empty(), push_back(), size(), front(), pop_front().

#### `void recordRate(const std::string &name, double value, std::chrono::seconds interval)`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:227
- Brief: Record a raw value sample for a rate metric.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `value` (double): Input parameter.
  - `interval` (std::chrono::seconds): Input parameter.
- Details: Record Rate. Unlike the integer-counter rate in MetricAggregator, this method accepts arbitrary double values. Samples older than interval are pruned on each call. name Metric name. value Raw sample value. interval Sliding window; samples older than this are discarded. name Input parameter. value Input parameter. interval Input parameter. Calls: lock(), std::chrono::steady_clock::now(), push_back(), count(), size(), front(), pop_front().

#### `void recordSummary(const std::string &name, double value)`
- Source: `include/observability/advanced_metrics.h`:117
- Brief: Record a value for a summary metric.
- Parameters:
  - `name` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `value` (double): Input parameter.
- Details: Record Summary. Up to kMaxSummarySamples most-recent observations are retained per metric name (oldest are evicted when the buffer is full). #### `void addBaggage(const std::string &key, const std::string &value)`
- Source: `include/observability/distributed_trace_span.h`:195
- Brief: Add a baggage item to this span (inherited by child spans).
- Parameters:
  - `key` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `value` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
- Details: Add Baggage. Baggage items are lightweight key-value pairs that propagate to child spans. Useful for passing context like user_id, tenant_id, or request priority without creating explicit span attributes. key Baggage key (required, non-empty). value Baggage value (can be empty). Thread-safe; multiple threads may add baggage concurrently. Maximum 128 baggage items per span (oldest inherited items are dropped). Baggage propagates automatically to child contexts created via childContext(). key Input parameter. value Input parameter. Calls: empty(), lock(), size().

#### `void addEvent(const std::string &event_name, const std::map< std::string, std::string > &attributes={})`
- Source: `include/observability/distributed_trace_span.h`:217
- Brief: Record an event within the span's lifetime.
- Parameters:

  - `attributes` (const std::map< std::string, std::string > &): Optional key-value pairs describing the event (e.g., {{"retry_count", "3"}}).
- Details: Events are lightweight markers (e.g., "cache_hit", "retry") with optional attributes and an automatic timestamp. event_name Name of the event (e.g., "cache_hit", "timeout_recovered"). attributes Optional key-value pairs describing the event (e.g., {{"retry_count", "3"}}). Thread-safe; multiple threads may record events concurrently. Events are stored inline; maximum 100 events per span. Examplespan->addEvent("query_start"); span->addEvent("cache_hit",{{"hit_rate","0.85"}}); span->addEvent("query_end",{{"duration_ms","42"},{"rows","10000"}});

New DistributedTraceContext ready for downstream propagation. std::invalid_argument if parent_context is invalid. This function is thread-safe. parent_context Input parameter. new_span_id Identifier of the new span. Return value. std::invalid_argument if an error occurs. Calls: validateTraceContext(), traceId(), traceState(), isTraceSampled(), empty(), generateSpanId(), lock(), baggage().

#### `void registerEventCallback(std::function< void(const std::vector< KernelEvent > &)> cb)`
- Source: `include/observability/ebpf_tracer.h`:197
- Brief: Register a callback invoked when at least one kernel event was collected in a cycle.
- Parameters:
  - `cb` (std::function< void(const std::vector< KernelEvent > &)>): n/a
- Details: The callback receives the KernelEvent objects collected during that cycle. It is called from the background thread; implementations must be thread-safe. The callback is NOT invoked on cycles where no new events were observed (e.g. zero deltas or non-Linux platforms).

- Source: `include/observability/metric_aggregator.h`:192
- Brief: Add a histogram snapshot to the internal buffer.

  - `snapshot` (const HistogramSnapshot &): Input parameter.
- Details: Add Histogram Snapshot. Snapshots are accumulated until aggregateHistograms() or reset() is called. Multiple snapshots with the same metric_name are merged. snapshot Input parameter. Calls: lock(), makeLabelFingerprint(), checkSnapshotCardinality(), makeSeriesKey(), push_back().

#### `AggregatedMetric aggregateHistograms(const std::string &metric_name, AggregationType type, const std::map< std::string, std::string > &filter_labels={}) const`
- Source: `include/observability/metric_aggregator.h`:206
- Brief: Aggregate all buffered snapshots for metric_name.
- Parameters:
  - `metric_name` (const std::string &): Metric to aggregate.
  - `type` (AggregationType): Reduction to apply (SUM/AVG/MAX/MIN/P50/P95/P99). RATE is not valid here; pass P99 instead.
  - `filter_labels` (const std::map< std::string, std::string > &): Only include snapshots whose label set contains all key/value pairs in filter_labels (empty = all).
- Return: AggregatedMetric with the computed value.
- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: if type is RATE or no snapshots are available for metric_name.
- Details: metric_name Metric to aggregate. type Reduction to apply (SUM/AVG/MAX/MIN/P50/P95/P99). RATE is not valid here; pass P99 instead. filter_labels Only include snapshots whose label set contains all key/value pairs in filter_labels (empty = all). AggregatedMetric with the computed value. std::invalid_argument if type is RATE or no snapshots are available for metric_name.

#### `ShardAggregationSnapshot aggregateShardMetrics(const std::vector< ShardMetrics > &shard_metrics) const`
- Source: `include/observability/metric_aggregator.h`:259
- Brief: Aggregate metrics from multiple shards into a single snapshot.
- Parameters:
  - `shard_metrics` (const std::vector< ShardMetrics > &): One ShardMetrics per shard.
- Return: ShardAggregationSnapshot containing one AggregatedMetric per applicable rule × group combination.
- Details: Converts each ShardMetrics into histogram snapshots (tagging them with the source shard_id), then applies all registered rules to produce a unified ShardAggregationSnapshot. This call does not mutate the internal snapshot buffer; the supplied shard data is processed transiently and the result is returned directly. shard_metrics One ShardMetrics per shard. ShardAggregationSnapshot containing one AggregatedMetric per applicable rule × group combination.

#### `void rollupMetrics(std::chrono::minutes window)`
- Source: `include/observability/metric_aggregator.h`:273
- Brief: Prune buffered histogram snapshots older than window.
- Parameters:
  - `window` (std::chrono::minutes): Input parameter.
- Details: Rollup Metrics. Reduces cardinality by discarding stale shard observations. Rate samples are also pruned with the same window (converted from minutes to seconds). Known-series tracking for cardinality limits is NOT reset so that new insertions continue to be validated against the same limits. window Retention window. Snapshots whose timestamp falls before now - window are discarded. window Input parameter. Calls: lock(), std::chrono::system_clock::now(), erase(), std::remove_if(), begin(), end(), empty(), std::chrono::steady_clock::now().

- Parameters:
  - `metric_name` (const std::string &): Name of the metric.
  - `limit` (size_t): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Metric Cardinality Limit. When the number of distinct label-set combinations for metric_name reaches limit, subsequent addHistogramSnapshot calls with new label sets are rejected (cardinality overflow is counted). Set to 0 to disable the limit for that metric. metric_name Name of the metric. limit Input parameter. Calls: lock().

#### `void pushMetrics(const MetricUpdate &update)`
- Source: `include/observability/metrics_stream_server.h`:254
- Brief: Fan out update to all matching subscriptions.
- Parameters:
  - `update` (const MetricUpdate &): Input parameter.
- Details: Push Metrics. For each active subscription, the method: Checks whether update.metric_name is in the subscription's metric_names list (empty list = subscribe to all). Evaluates all MetricFilter entries (AND semantics); if any filter does not match the update is skipped for that client. Enforces the per-subscription rate limit; if the last delivery to this client was less than update_interval ago the update is throttled. Serialises the update to JSON and invokes the SendFn callback. Thread-safe; may be called concurrently from multiple producer threads. update Input parameter. Calls: fetch_add(), load(), formatWebSocketMessage(), std::chrono::steady_clock::now(), lock(), matchesMetricNames(), matchesFilters(), count().

#### `void resetStats()`
- Source: `include/observability/metrics_stream_server.h`:288
- Brief: Reset Stats.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Reset all statistics counters to zero. Calls: store().

#### `void setDeliveryCallback(SendFn fn)`
- Source: `include/observability/metrics_stream_server.h`:169
- Brief: Register the delivery callback used to send serialised updates.
- Parameters:
  - `fn` (SendFn): Input parameter.
- Details: Set Delivery Callback. Must be set before the first call to pushMetrics() if actual delivery is required. Safe to replace at runtime. fn Callback invoked per eligible subscriber (may be null to disable delivery, e.g. during tests that only inspect stats). fn Input parameter. Calls: lock(), std::move().

#### `void start(const std::string &bind_address, uint16_t port)`
- Source: `include/observability/metrics_stream_server.h`:186
- Brief: Mark the server as running and record its bind coordinates.
- Parameters:
  - `bind_address` (const std::string &): Input parameter.
  - `port` (uint16_t): Input parameter.
- Throws:
  - std::runtime_error: if bind_address is empty or port is 0.
  - std::runtime_error: if an error occurs.
- Details: Start. In an embedded deployment the caller is responsible for creating the actual listening socket (e.g. a Boost.Beast WebSocket acceptor) and routing frames to subscribe() / unsubscribe() / pushMetrics(). This method records bind_address and port for informational purposes and sets the running flag. std::runtime_error if bind_address is empty or port is 0. bind_address Input parameter. port Input parameter. std::runtime_error if an error occurs. Calls: empty(), lock(), store().

#### `void stop()`
- Source: `include/observability/metrics_stream_server.h`:194
- Brief: Mark the server as stopped.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Stop. Existing subscriptions are preserved; no new deliveries are made after this call returns. Calls: store().

- Brief: Register or replace a client subscription.
- Parameters:

- Throws:
  - std::invalid_argument: if subscription.client_id is empty.
  - std::invalid_argument: if an error occurs.
- Details: Subscribe. If a subscription with the same client_id already exists it is overwritten. At most one subscription per client_id is supported. std::invalid_argument if subscription.client_id is empty. subscription Input parameter. std::invalid_argument if an error occurs. Calls: empty(), lock(), std::move().

### themis::observability::OperatorRemediationEngine

#### `OperatorRemediationEngine()`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:501
- Brief: Construct a remediation engine with built-in patterns.
- Parameters: none

#### `bool addListener(const std::shared_ptr< IRemediationHintListener > &listener)=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:532
- Brief: Add a listener for remediation hint events.
- Parameters:
  - `listener` (const std::shared_ptr< IRemediationHintListener > &): Listener implementation (stored as weak_ptr to prevent circular refs). Must not be null.
- Return: true on success, false if listener is null or listener count exceeds cap (default cap: 10k listeners, configured via memory pressure policy).
- Details: Registers a listener to receive notifications when hints are generated or resolved. listener Listener implementation (stored as weak_ptr to prevent circular refs). Must not be null. true on success, false if listener is null or listener count exceeds cap (default cap: 10k listeners, configured via memory pressure policy). @thread_safety Thread-safe. Multiple threads can call this concurrently. Listener addition is atomic; either fully added or rejected. Weak pointer semantics: listener can be deallocated externally without explicitly calling removeListener(). Stale listeners will be silently skipped during onNewHint() notification (weak_ptr upgrade fails). If listener count exceeds cap due to memory pressure, the oldest listeners are evicted in FIFO order before adding the new listener. Evicted listeners are notified via onHintResolved() with a special eviction hint ID. removeListener, getStatistics (for listener count)

#### `std::vector< std::shared_ptr< RemediationHint > > analyzeAndGenerateHints(const std::map< std::string, double > &metrics)=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:605
- Brief: Analyze current system metrics and generate remediation hints.
- Parameters:
  - `metrics` (const std::map< std::string, double > &): Map of metric name -> current value. Can be empty (returns empty hints vector).
- Return: Vector of newly-generated hints (empty if no problems detected or if hint generation is disabled via setHintGenerationEnabled(false)).
- Details: Runs all registered patterns against the supplied metrics and generates hints for any patterns that match. Duplicate hints (same problem detected within deduplication window) are filtered; only new unique problems are emitted to listeners. metrics Map of metric name -> current value. Can be empty (returns empty hints vector). Vector of newly-generated hints (empty if no problems detected or if hint generation is disabled via setHintGenerationEnabled(false)). @thread_safety Thread-safe. Multiple threads can call this concurrently. Pattern matching and deduplication use reader locks. For each matching pattern: Pattern::match() is called (must complete in < 10ms) Hint is checked against deduplication window If unique, hint is added to active set and listeners notified If duplicate, deduplication counter incremented (no listener notification) If any listener notification fails (listener deallocated or listener throws), error is logged but other listeners continue to be notified. Malformed input (null metric names, invalid categories) is rejected with ORE_INVALID_METRIC_DATA error code and skipped (no hint generated). registerPattern, setHintGenerationEnabled, setDeduplicationWindow

#### `void clearAllHints()=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:730
- Brief: Clear all hint history.
- Parameters: none
- Details: Removes all generated hints and resets deduplication tracking. This function is thread-safe.

#### `std::vector< std::shared_ptr< RemediationHint > > getActiveHints()=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:624
- Brief: Get list of currently-active hints.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Vector of active hints (empty if no active hints exist).
- Details: Returns a snapshot of all hints that have been generated and not yet resolved. Vector of active hints (empty if no active hints exist). @thread_safety Thread-safe but results are snapshots. The returned vector is a point-in-time copy and may become stale immediately after the call returns (new hints may be generated, others resolved). For consistency with concurrent generation, prefer category or severity filtering methods (getHintsByCategory, getHintsBySeverity) when possible. getHintById, getHintsByCategory, getHintsBySeverity, resolveHint

- Details: Current deduplication window duration. This function is thread-safe.

#### `std::map< std::string, double > getStatistics()=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:754
- Brief: Get statistics about hint generation.
- Parameters: none
- Return: Map with keys as above. Empty string keys are not included.
- Details: Returns a snapshot of statistics including: "total_hints_generated": Total hints created (including duplicates) "active_hints": Currently-active (unresolved) hints "hints_by_severity_info": Count of INFO severity hints "hints_by_severity_warning": Count of WARNING severity hints "hints_by_severity_critical": Count of CRITICAL severity hints "listener_count": Current active listener count "listener_eviction_count": Total listeners evicted due to memory pressure "duplicate_suppression_count": Total duplicates filtered by deduplication window "pattern_registration_errors": Total failed pattern registrations "listener_notification_failures": Total listener notification errors Map with keys as above. Empty string keys are not included. @thread_safety Thread-safe but results are snapshots. Counters are atomic but may change immediately after the call returns. getActiveHints, setDeduplicationWindow

- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:553
- Brief: Remove a listener.

Returns false if listener is null or not in the active set. @thread_safety Thread-safe. Multiple threads can call this concurrently. Removal is atomic; listener will not receive notifications after this call returns true. This is optional: listeners can be deallocated without calling removeListener(). The engine uses weak pointers so stale listeners are handled gracefully. addListener, getActiveHints

#### `void setDeduplicationWindow(std::chrono::seconds window)=0`
- Source: `include/observability/operator_remediation_engine.h`:712
- Brief: Set the deduplication time window for hints.
- Parameters:
  - `window` (std::chrono::seconds): Time window duration. Recommended: 5 minutes (default). Must be > 0. Edge cases: Very short windows (< 1s): high duplicate traffic Very long windows (> 1h): alerts suppressed for too long
- Details: Hints with the same category and title generated within this time window are considered duplicates and only the first is emitted to listeners. window Time window duration. Recommended: 5 minutes (default). Must be > 0. Edge cases: Very short windows (< 1s): high duplicate traffic Very long windows (> 1h): alerts suppressed for too long @thread_safety Thread-safe. Window change applies to all future hints. In-flight deduplication checks may use old or new window (last-writer-wins semantics). Window rounding is done on hint generation timestamp: timestamp_rounded = floor(current_time / window_size) * window_size This ensures clock skew tolerance of ±(window_size/2) ≈ ±2.5 minutes for default 5-minute window. getDeduplicationWindow, analyzeAndGenerateHints

- Brief: Enable/disable automatic hint generation.
- Parameters:
  - `enabled` (bool): true to enable hint generation, false to disable.
- Details: When disabled, analyzeAndGenerateHints() returns empty vector. enabled true to enable hint generation, false to disable. This function is thread-safe.

For each matching pattern: Pattern::match() is called (must complete in < 10ms) Hint is checked against deduplication window If unique, hint is added to active set and listeners notified If duplicate, deduplication counter incremented (no listener notification) If any listener notification fails (listener deallocated or listener throws), error is logged but other listeners continue to be notified. Malformed input (null metric names, invalid categories) is rejected with ORE_INVALID_METRIC_DATA error code and skipped (no hint generated). registerPattern, setHintGenerationEnabled, setDeduplicationWindow

Vector of active hints (empty if no active hints exist). @thread_safety Thread-safe but results are snapshots. The returned vector is a point-in-time copy and may become stale immediately after the call returns (new hints may be generated, others resolved). For consistency with concurrent generation, prefer category or severity filtering methods (getHintsByCategory, getHintsBySeverity) when possible. getHintById, getHintsByCategory, getHintsBySeverity, resolveHint

#### `std::map< std::string, double > getStatistics() override`
- Source: `src/observability/operator_remediation_engine.cpp`:708
- Brief: Get statistics about hint generation.

- Details: Returns a snapshot of statistics including: "total_hints_generated": Total hints created (including duplicates) "active_hints": Currently-active (unresolved) hints "hints_by_severity_info": Count of INFO severity hints "hints_by_severity_warning": Count of WARNING severity hints "hints_by_severity_critical": Count of CRITICAL severity hints "listener_count": Current active listener count "listener_eviction_count": Total listeners evicted due to memory pressure "duplicate_suppression_count": Total duplicates filtered by deduplication window "pattern_registration_errors": Total failed pattern registrations "listener_notification_failures": Total listener notification errors Map with keys as above. Empty string keys are not included. @thread_safety Thread-safe but results are snapshots. Counters are atomic but may change immediately after the call returns. getActiveHints, setDeduplicationWindow

Returns false if listener is null or not in the active set. @thread_safety Thread-safe. Multiple threads can call this concurrently. Removal is atomic; listener will not receive notifications after this call returns true. This is optional: listeners can be deallocated without calling removeListener(). The engine uses weak pointers so stale listeners are handled gracefully. addListener, getActiveHints

#### `void setDeduplicationWindow(std::chrono::seconds window) override`
- Source: `src/observability/operator_remediation_engine.cpp`:678
- Brief: Set the deduplication time window for hints.
- Parameters:
  - `window` (std::chrono::seconds): Time window duration. Recommended: 5 minutes (default). Must be > 0. Edge cases: Very short windows (< 1s): high duplicate traffic Very long windows (> 1h): alerts suppressed for too long
- Details: Hints with the same category and title generated within this time window are considered duplicates and only the first is emitted to listeners. window Time window duration. Recommended: 5 minutes (default). Must be > 0. Edge cases: Very short windows (< 1s): high duplicate traffic Very long windows (> 1h): alerts suppressed for too long @thread_safety Thread-safe. Window change applies to all future hints. In-flight deduplication checks may use old or new window (last-writer-wins semantics). Window rounding is done on hint generation timestamp: timestamp_rounded = floor(current_time / window_size) * window_size This ensures clock skew tolerance of ±(window_size/2) ≈ ±2.5 minutes for default 5-minute window. getDeduplicationWindow, analyzeAndGenerateHints

- Brief: Enable/disable automatic hint generation.
- Parameters:
  - `enabled` (bool): true to enable hint generation, false to disable.
- Details: When disabled, analyzeAndGenerateHints() returns empty vector. enabled true to enable hint generation, false to disable. This function is thread-safe.

Applies a simple Granger-inspired heuristic: if metric A consistently leads metric B by ~1 lag step with a strong correlation, an edge A→B is added. metrics Time series to analyse. A CausalGraph suitable for rendering or traversal. metrics Input parameter. Return value. Calls: push_back(), size(), extractValues(), alignVectors(), pearsonCorrelation(), a_lag(), begin(), end().

Calls: find(), end(), extractValues(), alignVectors(), size(), pearsonCorrelation(), std::abs(), std::string().

- Return: Burn rate, or 0.0 if there are no samples in the window.
- Details: samples Complete sample deque (already expired to the full window). window Sub-window duration to evaluate. now Reference point (wall clock at evaluation time). allowed_error_rate = 1 - slo.objective Burn rate, or 0.0 if there are no samples in the window.
